# Added by OrbStack: 'orb' SSH host for Linux machines
# This only works if it's at the top of ssh_config (before any Host blocks).
# This won't be added again if you remove it.
Include ~/.orbstack/ssh/config

Include ~/.ssh/sealos/devbox_config

# 全局禁用代理（确保直连）
# 2026-10-06: 移除 SetEnv 注入的代理变量 —— SSH 会话保持纯直连，
# 远端需要外网代理时由服务器端按需函数提供（codex-a/codex-b、clash 等）


# ── linux-dev 带外管理通道（2026-10-13 新增）────────────────────────────────
# 背景：ZeroTier 的 P2P 打洞会失效（卡中继 / IPv6 路径抖动），那时 10.111.127.12 完全不通。
# 但 linux-dev 有一条**主动外连** sanjiaozhou 的反向隧道（面板就走它，不受 NAT 影响），
# 于是复用同一条隧道把它的 SSH 也转出来：linux-dev 上 systemd `ssh-reverse-tunnel.service`
# 执行 `-R 127.0.0.1:18098:localhost:22`。
# ⇒ 只要 sanjiaozhou 可达，就能进 linux-dev，**与 ZeroTier 无关**。
# 🔴 本块必须放在 `Host *` **之前**：OpenSSH 对每个参数取**第一个**匹配值，
#    而 `Host *` 设了 `ProxyCommand none`，放它后面会被忽略（实测 Connection refused）。
# 🔴 不能用 ProxyJump：它与 ProxyCommand 互斥且后者优先 ⇒ 跳板被忽略、直连本机 127.0.0.1。
Host linux-dev-oob
    HostName 127.0.0.1
    Port 18098
    User xaiver
    IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
    IdentitiesOnly yes
    ProxyCommand ssh -W %h:%p sanjiaozhou
    ConnectTimeout 25
    ServerAliveInterval 20

Host *
  ProxyCommand none

Host lingchuang
  HostName 39.107.226.94
  User admin
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/xaiver.pem
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  StrictHostKeyChecking accept-new
  ServerAliveInterval 60
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

Host sanjiaozhou
  HostName 101.34.250.109
  User root
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/sanjiaozhou.pem
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ServerAliveInterval 20
  ServerAliveCountMax 30

Host ubuntu-jcli finlaw
  HostName 124.223.13.226
  User ubuntu
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/ubuntu-jcli.pem
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ProxyCommand none
  ControlMaster auto
  ControlPersist 10m
  ControlPath ~/.ssh/cm-%C
  ServerAliveInterval 60
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

# 用途：让 SSOS 项目涉及的本地 Mac / Windows 打包机 / 另一台 Mac
#       三台机器在不同网络环境下也能通过固定虚拟 IP 互 SSH。

#   - windows-pc (Win11):  10.111.127.237  (节点 7578ffc5b6)  — Windows 端打包构建
#                          主机名 邓湘雷-win / SSH 用户 41478 / 局域网 192.168.1.3
#                          （2026-09 系统重装后新身份；旧值 10.111.127.156 / ed4d554295 已失效）
#
# Windows 打包机换网络/换地方后，不需要改 SSH config，只需要：
#   1. 保持 Windows 上 ZeroTier One 运行；
#   2. 本机 Mac 上 ZeroTier One 运行；
#   3. 在 https://my.zerotier.com/network/166359304e354777 保持节点 Auth。

Host windows-pc

===== scripts/deploy.sh =====
#!/bin/bash
set -e

# ============================================================
# CD 部署脚本 - xiangleideng.site
# 由 GitHub Actions 通过 SSH 触发执行
# 服务器路径: /opt/home/deploy.sh
# 用法: bash deploy.sh [--skip-build]
# ============================================================

REPO_DIR="/opt/home"
DEPLOY_SITE_ID="personal-home"
LOG_FILE="$REPO_DIR/deploy.log"
TIMESTAMP=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')

RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
NC='\033[0m'

log()    { echo -e "${GREEN}[$TIMESTAMP] $1${NC}" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"; }
warn()   { echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARN] $1${NC}" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"; }
error()  { echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] $1${NC}" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"; exit 1; }
step()   { echo -e "${BLUE}===> $1${NC}" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"; }

verify_deploy_target() {
  local resolved_dir marker actual_site_id
  resolved_dir="$(readlink -f "$REPO_DIR")"
  marker="$REPO_DIR/.deploy-site-id"

  if [ "$resolved_dir" != "/opt/home" ]; then
    error "拒绝部署：个人站目录解析为 $resolved_dir，而不是 /opt/home"
  fi

  if [ -f "$marker" ]; then
    actual_site_id="$(cat "$marker")"
    if [ "$actual_site_id" != "$DEPLOY_SITE_ID" ]; then
      error "拒绝部署：目标目录属于另一个站点 ($actual_site_id)"
    fi
  fi

  printf '%s\n' "$DEPLOY_SITE_ID" > "$marker"
}

verify_deploy_target

# 解析参数
SKIP_BUILD=false
for arg in "$@"; do
  case $arg in
    --skip-build) SKIP_BUILD=true ;;
  esac
done

echo "" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"
log "========== CI/CD 自动部署开始 =========="

if [ "$SKIP_BUILD" = true ]; then
  log "跳过 Git 同步和构建步骤 (--skip-build，使用本地上传的文件)"
  if [ -d "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist" ]; then
    mkdir -p "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin"
    rm -rf "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin/"*
    cp -r "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist/"* "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin/"
    log "已同步本地 blog-admin 构建产物"
  fi
else
  # ---- Step 1: 拉取最新代码 ----
  step "Step 1/6: 拉取最新代码"
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  git fetch origin --quiet
  LOCAL=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
  REMOTE=$(git rev-parse origin/dev)

  if [ "$LOCAL" = "$REMOTE" ]; then
    warn "代码无变化 ($(git rev-parse --short HEAD))，跳过部署"
    log "========== 无需部署 =========="
    exit 0
  fi

  # 强制对齐远程（丢弃本地修改，确保部署一致性）
  git reset --hard origin/dev 2>&1 | tail -3
  log "代码已更新: $(git rev-parse --short HEAD) — $(git log -1 --pretty='%s')"

  # ---- Step 2: 安装依赖 ----
  step "Step 2/6: 安装依赖"
  export npm_config_registry=https://registry.npmmirror.com
  pnpm install --frozen-lockfile 2>&1 | tail -10
  log "依赖安装完成"

  # ---- Step 3: 构建应用 ----
  step "Step 3/6: 构建所有应用"

  # 3a. homepage
  log "构建 homepage..."
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  pnpm --filter homepage build 2>&1 | tail -5
  log "homepage 构建完成"

  # 3b. blog-admin (生产模式)
  log "构建 blog-admin (生产模式)..."
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  pnpm --filter blog-admin build:pro 2>&1 | tail -5
  # 复制到 blog-api public 目录，让 Express 托管静态文件
  mkdir -p "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin"
  rm -rf "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin/"*
  cp -r "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist/"* "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin/"
  log "blog-admin 构建完成，已复制到 blog-api/public/admin/"

  # 3c. blog-frontend (Nuxt3 SSR)
  log "构建 blog-frontend (Nuxt3 SSR)..."
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  pnpm --filter blog-frontend build 2>&1 | tail -10
  log "blog-frontend 构建完成"

  # ---- Step 3.5: 数据库迁移 ----
  step "Step 3.5/6: 数据库迁移"
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  if pnpm --filter blog-api db:migrate 2>&1 | tail -5; then
    log "数据库迁移完成"
  else
    warn "数据库迁移失败 (可能无新迁移或数据库未就绪)"
  fi
fi

# 将生产对象存储等运行时变量注入当前部署进程和 PM2。
if [ -f "$REPO_DIR/.env.pro" ]; then
  set -a
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  . "$REPO_DIR/.env.pro"
  set +a
fi

# ---- Step 4: 重启后端服务 ----
step "Step 4/6: 重启后端服务"

# blog-api (spaceP_pro)
if pm2 list | grep -q "spaceP_pro"; then
  pm2 restart spaceP_pro --update-env 2>&1 | tail -3
else
  cd "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api"
  pm2 start ecosystem.config.js --env pro 2>&1 | tail -5
fi

# music-api (Meting 代理)
if pm2 list | grep -q "music-api"; then
  pm2 restart music-api 2>&1 | tail -3
else
  cd "$REPO_DIR/services/music-api"
  pm2 start ecosystem.config.js --env pro 2>&1 | tail -5 2>/dev/null || {
    pm2 start "node dist/index.js" \
      --name music-api \
      --cwd "$REPO_DIR/services/music-api" \
      2>&1 | tail -5
  }
fi

# ws-gateway (通用 WebSocket 网关)
export PORT=4010
export NODE_ENV=pro
export BLOG_API_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8085/api
export WS_PATH=/ws
if [ -z "$JWT_SECRET" ]; then
  JWT_SECRET=$(cd "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api" && NODE_ENV=pro node -e "const config=require('config'); process.stdout.write(config.get('tokenSecretKey') || '')")
  export JWT_SECRET
fi
if [ -z "$JWT_SECRET" ]; then
  warn "JWT_SECRET 未设置，ws-gateway 将无法启动"
fi
if pm2 list | grep -q "ws-gateway"; then
  pm2 restart ws-gateway --update-env 2>&1 | tail -3
else
  cd "$REPO_DIR/services/ws-gateway"
  pm2 start "node index.js" \
    --name ws-gateway \
    --cwd "$REPO_DIR/services/ws-gateway" \
    2>&1 | tail -5
fi

sleep 2

# blog-frontend Nuxt3 SSR
if pm2 list | grep -q "blog-frontend"; then
  pm2 restart blog-frontend 2>&1 | tail -3
else
  cd "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-frontend"
  pm2 start ecosystem.config.cjs --env pro 2>&1 | tail -5 2>/dev/null || {
    pm2 start "PORT=3004 node .output/server/index.mjs" \
      --name blog-frontend \
      --cwd "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-frontend" \
      2>&1 | tail -5
  }
fi

sleep 2

# ---- Step 5: 重载 Nginx ----
step "Step 5/6: 重载 Nginx"
if sudo /usr/sbin/nginx -t 2>&1 && sudo /usr/sbin/nginx -s reload 2>&1; then
  log "Nginx 重载成功"
else
  warn "Nginx 重载失败，请检查配置"
fi

# ---- Step 6: 健康检查 ----
step "Step 6/6: 健康检查"

check_service() {
  local name=$1 url=$2
  local code=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' --max-time 5 "$url" 2>/dev/null || echo "000")
  if [[ "$code" =~ ^[23] ]]; then
    log "  ✓ $name: HTTP $code"
  else
    warn "  ✗ $name: HTTP $code (可能正在启动)"
  fi
}

check_service "blog-api"       "http://127.0.0.1:8085/api/article/reception/getArticleByTypeId/0/1/5"
check_service "blog-frontend"  "http://127.0.0.1:3004/blog/"
check_service "homepage"       "http://127.0.0.1:8085/admin/"  # /admin 走 blog-api 静态托管
check_service "music-api"      "http://127.0.0.1:4000"
check_service "ws-gateway"     "http://127.0.0.1:4010/health"
warn "若公网 WebSocket 不可用，请检查 Nginx /ws 是否已配置 Upgrade 反向代理"

log "========== 部署完成 =========="
log "提交: $(git -C "$REPO_DIR" log -1 --pretty='%h %s')"
echo ""

#### Tool: Bash

**Input:**

```json
{
  "command": "cat ~/.ssh/config 2>/dev/null | head -100",
  "description": "Read SSH config"
}
```

**Response:**

# Added by OrbStack: 'orb' SSH host for Linux machines
# This only works if it's at the top of ssh_config (before any Host blocks).
# This won't be added again if you remove it.
Include ~/.orbstack/ssh/config

Include ~/.ssh/sealos/devbox_config

# 全局禁用代理（确保直连）
# 2026-10-06: 移除 SetEnv 注入的代理变量 —— SSH 会话保持纯直连，
# 远端需要外网代理时由服务器端按需函数提供（codex-a/codex-b、clash 等）


# ── linux-dev 带外管理通道（2026-10-13 新增）────────────────────────────────
# 背景：ZeroTier 的 P2P 打洞会失效（卡中继 / IPv6 路径抖动），那时 10.111.127.12 完全不通。
# 但 linux-dev 有一条**主动外连** sanjiaozhou 的反向隧道（面板就走它，不受 NAT 影响），
# 于是复用同一条隧道把它的 SSH 也转出来：linux-dev 上 systemd `ssh-reverse-tunnel.service`
# 执行 `-R 127.0.0.1:18098:localhost:22`。
# ⇒ 只要 sanjiaozhou 可达，就能进 linux-dev，**与 ZeroTier 无关**。
# 🔴 本块必须放在 `Host *` **之前**：OpenSSH 对每个参数取**第一个**匹配值，
#    而 `Host *` 设了 `ProxyCommand none`，放它后面会被忽略（实测 Connection refused）。
# 🔴 不能用 ProxyJump：它与 ProxyCommand 互斥且后者优先 ⇒ 跳板被忽略、直连本机 127.0.0.1。
Host linux-dev-oob
    HostName 127.0.0.1
    Port 18098
    User xaiver
    IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
    IdentitiesOnly yes
    ProxyCommand ssh -W %h:%p sanjiaozhou
    ConnectTimeout 25
    ServerAliveInterval 20

Host *
  ProxyCommand none

Host lingchuang
  HostName 39.107.226.94
  User admin
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/xaiver.pem
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  StrictHostKeyChecking accept-new
  ServerAliveInterval 60
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

Host sanjiaozhou
  HostName 101.34.250.109
  User root
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/sanjiaozhou.pem
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ServerAliveInterval 20
  ServerAliveCountMax 30

Host ubuntu-jcli finlaw
  HostName 124.223.13.226
  User ubuntu
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/ubuntu-jcli.pem
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ProxyCommand none
  ControlMaster auto
  ControlPersist 10m
  ControlPath ~/.ssh/cm-%C
  ServerAliveInterval 60
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

# ZeroTier 虚拟局域网组网说明
# Network ID: 166359304e354777
# 用途：让 SSOS 项目涉及的本地 Mac / Windows 打包机 / 另一台 Mac
#       三台机器在不同网络环境下也能通过固定虚拟 IP 互 SSH。
#
# 成员：
#   - 本地 Mac:            10.111.127.246  (节点 76:21:8e:d0:8b:75)
#   - windows-pc (Win11):  10.111.127.237  (节点 7578ffc5b6)  — Windows 端打包构建
#                          主机名 邓湘雷-win / SSH 用户 41478 / 局域网 192.168.1.3
#                          （2026-09 系统重装后新身份；旧值 10.111.127.156 / ed4d554295 已失效）
#
# Windows 打包机换网络/换地方后，不需要改 SSH config，只需要：
#   1. 保持 Windows 上 ZeroTier One 运行；
#   2. 本机 Mac 上 ZeroTier One 运行；
#   3. 在 https://my.zerotier.com/network/166359304e354777 保持节点 Auth。
#
# 如果新增机器，在该机器上执行：
#   sudo zerotier-cli join 166359304e354777
# 然后去 ZeroTier Central 授权即可。

Host windows-pc
  HostName 10.111.127.237
  User 41478
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ProxyCommand none
  ServerAliveInterval 60
  ServerAliveCountMax 3
  # Windows 打包服务器 - 用于各项目的 Windows 端打包构建
  # ZeroTier 虚拟 IP，Windows 机器换网络/换地方后依然可用
  # 代理配置：http://127.0.0.1:7890
  # 2026-09 系统重装后：主机名 邓湘雷-win，账户 41478，局域网 192.168.1.3

===== scripts/deploy.sh =====
#!/bin/bash
set -e

# ============================================================
# CD 部署脚本 - xiangleideng.site
# 由 GitHub Actions 通过 SSH 触发执行
# 服务器路径: /opt/home/deploy.sh
# 用法: bash deploy.sh [--skip-build]
# ============================================================

REPO_DIR="/opt/home"
DEPLOY_SITE_ID="personal-home"
LOG_FILE="$REPO_DIR/deploy.log"
TIMESTAMP=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')

RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
NC='\033[0m'

log()    { echo -e "${GREEN}[$TIMESTAMP] $1${NC}" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"; }
warn()   { echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARN] $1${NC}" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"; }
error()  { echo -e "${RED}[ERROR] $1${NC}" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"; exit 1; }
step()   { echo -e "${BLUE}===> $1${NC}" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"; }

verify_deploy_target() {
  local resolved_dir marker actual_site_id
  resolved_dir="$(readlink -f "$REPO_DIR")"
  marker="$REPO_DIR/.deploy-site-id"

  if [ "$resolved_dir" != "/opt/home" ]; then
    error "拒绝部署：个人站目录解析为 $resolved_dir，而不是 /opt/home"
  fi

  if [ -f "$marker" ]; then
    actual_site_id="$(cat "$marker")"
    if [ "$actual_site_id" != "$DEPLOY_SITE_ID" ]; then
      error "拒绝部署：目标目录属于另一个站点 ($actual_site_id)"
    fi
  fi

  printf '%s\n' "$DEPLOY_SITE_ID" > "$marker"
}

verify_deploy_target

# 解析参数
SKIP_BUILD=false
for arg in "$@"; do
  case $arg in
    --skip-build) SKIP_BUILD=true ;;
  esac
done

echo "" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"
log "========== CI/CD 自动部署开始 =========="

if [ "$SKIP_BUILD" = true ]; then
  log "跳过 Git 同步和构建步骤 (--skip-build，使用本地上传的文件)"
  if [ -d "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist" ]; then
    mkdir -p "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin"
    rm -rf "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin/"*
    cp -r "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist/"* "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin/"
    log "已同步本地 blog-admin 构建产物"
  fi
else
  # ---- Step 1: 拉取最新代码 ----
  step "Step 1/6: 拉取最新代码"
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  git fetch origin --quiet
  LOCAL=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
  REMOTE=$(git rev-parse origin/dev)

  if [ "$LOCAL" = "$REMOTE" ]; then
    warn "代码无变化 ($(git rev-parse --short HEAD))，跳过部署"
    log "========== 无需部署 =========="
    exit 0
  fi

  # 强制对齐远程（丢弃本地修改，确保部署一致性）
  git reset --hard origin/dev 2>&1 | tail -3
  log "代码已更新: $(git rev-parse --short HEAD) — $(git log -1 --pretty='%s')"

  # ---- Step 2: 安装依赖 ----
  step "Step 2/6: 安装依赖"
  export npm_config_registry=https://registry.npmmirror.com
  pnpm install --frozen-lockfile 2>&1 | tail -10
  log "依赖安装完成"

  # ---- Step 3: 构建应用 ----
  step "Step 3/6: 构建所有应用"

  # 3a. homepage
  log "构建 homepage..."
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  pnpm --filter homepage build 2>&1 | tail -5
  log "homepage 构建完成"

  # 3b. blog-admin (生产模式)
  log "构建 blog-admin (生产模式)..."
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  pnpm --filter blog-admin build:pro 2>&1 | tail -5
  # 复制到 blog-api public 目录，让 Express 托管静态文件
  mkdir -p "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin"
  rm -rf "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin/"*
  cp -r "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist/"* "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/admin/"
  log "blog-admin 构建完成，已复制到 blog-api/public/admin/"

  # 3c. blog-frontend (Nuxt3 SSR)
  log "构建 blog-frontend (Nuxt3 SSR)..."
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  pnpm --filter blog-frontend build 2>&1 | tail -10
  log "blog-frontend 构建完成"

  # ---- Step 3.5: 数据库迁移 ----
  step "Step 3.5/6: 数据库迁移"
  cd "$REPO_DIR"
  if pnpm --filter blog-api db:migrate 2>&1 | tail -5; then
    log "数据库迁移完成"
  else
    warn "数据库迁移失败 (可能无新迁移或数据库未就绪)"
  fi
fi

# 将生产对象存储等运行时变量注入当前部署进程和 PM2。
if [ -f "$REPO_DIR/.env.pro" ]; then
  set -a
  # shellcheck disable=SC1091
  . "$REPO_DIR/.env.pro"
  set +a
fi

# ---- Step 4: 重启后端服务 ----
step "Step 4/6: 重启后端服务"

# blog-api (spaceP_pro)
if pm2 list | grep -q "spaceP_pro"; then
  pm2 restart spaceP_pro --update-env 2>&1 | tail -3
else
  cd "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api"
  pm2 start ecosystem.config.js --env pro 2>&1 | tail -5
fi

# music-api (Meting 代理)
if pm2 list | grep -q "music-api"; then
  pm2 restart music-api 2>&1 | tail -3
else
  cd "$REPO_DIR/services/music-api"
  pm2 start ecosystem.config.js --env pro 2>&1 | tail -5 2>/dev/null || {
    pm2 start "node dist/index.js" \
      --name music-api \
      --cwd "$REPO_DIR/services/music-api" \
      2>&1 | tail -5
  }
fi

# ws-gateway (通用 WebSocket 网关)
export PORT=4010
export NODE_ENV=pro
export BLOG_API_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8085/api
export WS_PATH=/ws
if [ -z "$JWT_SECRET" ]; then
  JWT_SECRET=$(cd "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api" && NODE_ENV=pro node -e "const config=require('config'); process.stdout.write(config.get('tokenSecretKey') || '')")
  export JWT_SECRET
fi
if [ -z "$JWT_SECRET" ]; then
  warn "JWT_SECRET 未设置，ws-gateway 将无法启动"
fi
if pm2 list | grep -q "ws-gateway"; then
  pm2 restart ws-gateway --update-env 2>&1 | tail -3
else
  cd "$REPO_DIR/services/ws-gateway"
  pm2 start "node index.js" \
    --name ws-gateway \
    --cwd "$REPO_DIR/services/ws-gateway" \
    2>&1 | tail -5
fi

sleep 2

# blog-frontend Nuxt3 SSR
if pm2 list | grep -q "blog-frontend"; then
  pm2 restart blog-frontend 2>&1 | tail -3
else
  cd "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-frontend"
  pm2 start ecosystem.config.cjs --env pro 2>&1 | tail -5 2>/dev/null || {
    pm2 start "PORT=3004 node .output/server/index.mjs" \
      --name blog-frontend \
      --cwd "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-frontend" \
      2>&1 | tail -5
  }
fi

sleep 2

# ---- Step 5: 重载 Nginx ----
step "Step 5/6: 重载 Nginx"
if sudo /usr/sbin/nginx -t 2>&1 && sudo /usr/sbin/nginx -s reload 2>&1; then
  log "Nginx 重载成功"
else
  warn "Nginx 重载失败，请检查配置"
fi

# ---- Step 6: 健康检查 ----
step "Step 6/6: 健康检查"

check_service() {
  local name=$1 url=$2
  local code=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' --max-time 5 "$url" 2>/dev/null || echo "000")
  if [[ "$code" =~ ^[23] ]]; then
    log "  ✓ $name: HTTP $code"
  else
    warn "  ✗ $name: HTTP $code (可能正在启动)"
  fi
}

check_service "blog-api"       "http://127.0.0.1:8085/api/article/reception/getArticleByTypeId/0/1/5"
check_service "blog-frontend"  "http://127.0.0.1:3004/blog/"
check_service "homepage"       "http://127.0.0.1:8085/admin/"  # /admin 走 blog-api 静态托管
check_service "music-api"      "http://127.0.0.1:4000"
check_service "ws-gateway"     "http://127.0.0.1:4010/health"
warn "若公网 WebSocket 不可用，请检查 Nginx /ws 是否已配置 Upgrade 反向代理"

log "========== 部署完成 =========="
log "提交: $(git -C "$REPO_DIR" log -1 --pretty='%h %s')"
echo ""

#### Thinking

The user clarifies: they mean deploying to a different server (the hongkong one, 45.207.214.159), i.e., move deployment off the old server onto this new one. And change the domain to xaiverdeng.site.

So the task: deploy the project to the hongkong server as a new deployment target, with domain xaiverdeng.site.

Key facts already gathered:
- Server: Ubuntu 24.04, x86_64, 16 vCPU, 15GB RAM, 59GB free disk. Has git, docker, caddy (serving wunoai.com sites on 80/443), NO node/pnpm/pm2/nginx/mysql/redis.
- DNS: xaiverdeng.site + www already point to 45.207.214.159. Old domain xiangleideng.site is expired (→ domainexpired).
- Repo: pnpm monorepo; prod stack expects MySQL (space_log_blog), Redis, MinIO, PM2, nginx reverse proxy; ports blog-api 8085, blog-frontend 3004/3001, music-api 3000, ws-gateway 4010, homepage static.
- Domain appears in ~140 places across config, source, docs.

This is a big task. Before I start making changes on a shared production server that hosts another project (wunoai.com ERPNext + WeKnora), I need to decide the approach and get key decisions from the user — or make sensible product-level decisions.

Memory guidance: user prefers I don't ask for authorization at every step; decide ops defaults myself with evidence + one rollback line. But there are genuine questions here that only the user can answer:

1. Data migration: is there existing blog data (articles, users, uploads) on the OLD server that must be migrated? Which old server? The old one was xiangleideng.site which is now expired. I need to find the old server's SSH host. Let me check ssh config for it and .central/credentials.env for DEPLOY_SSH_HOST.

2. Caddy vs Nginx on the new server: existing Caddy occupies 80/443 and serves wunoai.com. Options: (a) add site blocks to existing Caddy (recommended, no port conflict, auto-SSL via Let's Encrypt — and xaiverdeng.site DNS already points here, so Caddy can get certs automatically), (b) install nginx and move caddy off ports (risky, would break wunoai.com). I should decide (a) myself — that's an ops default with clear evidence. Memory says 可代拍的运维默认值: 现量依据+一条回退+标明代拍.

3. MySQL/Redis/MinIO: install natively vs docker. Docker is already present and heavily used. Given existing docker, running mysql/redis/minio via docker-compose is clean and isolated from the existing containers. But the repo's deploy scripts assume PM2 + native. Actually PM2 for node apps is still fine; DB in docker is fine (host ports 127.0.0.1:3306).

4. Whether this server also hosts wunoai.com — is it OK to put the personal site there? It's the user's call about co-tenancy, but they already said deploy here. So proceed.

5. ICP: old site had 湘ICP备2026026942号-1. HK server — no ICP needed for hosting, but the footer ICP filing is tied to the domain registration. Domain change: is xaiverdeng.site filed under the same ICP? That's a compliance question — I should flag, not decide. Actually, memory has heavy context about ICP/公安备案 workflows. The ICP number 湘ICP备2026026942号-1 — if the new domain isn't included in the filing, displaying it would be a false statement. This is an outward legal representation → must ask/flag. Per memory: 对外法律表征 needs confirmation.

Let me first find the old server to know whether data needs migrating. Check .central/credentials.env and ssh config.

Let me gather that before asking questions. Also check whether there's an existing deploy target env.

Let me look at ssh config remainder (lines 100-260) and .central/credentials.env.

# LAPTOP-5UEUU7PS；通过 ZeroTier 直连，使用 Ed25519 公钥认证。
Host laptop-5ueuu7ps windows-codex
  HostName 10.111.127.151
  User rayne
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ProxyCommand none
  ServerAliveInterval 60
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

# wuno（WunoOS）生产服务器 —— 🔴 2026-10-24 已换机，勿改回旧 IP
# 新机：腾讯云 175.178.166.102（hostname VM-0-10-ubuntu，Ubuntu 26.04，8C/15G/266G）
#   docker compose 项目 `erpnext-wuno`，根 /opt/wunoos-frappe，站点 erp.localhost
# 🔴 与旧机唯一不同的一半：SSH 用户是 **ubuntu**，不是 root —— 用 root 会 Permission denied
# 🔴 另一半：钥匙换成 RSA ~/.ssh/wuno.pem（旧 ed25519 只认旧机，别再拿它连新机）
# ⚠️ 本机所有 wuno 部署脚本（scripts/deploy/ssh-release.sh、qa/crm-forged-context.probe.sh 等）
#    都只认这个别名；换机只需改这一段，脚本一行都不用动。
Host wunoos wuno
  HostName 175.178.166.102
  User ubuntu
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/wuno.pem
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ServerAliveInterval 60
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

# 旧机（华为云 ecs-249c，119.8.167.61）—— 🔴 仅保留用于回滚／核对，不要再往上部署
# 2026-10-24 现量：端口 22 仍 OPEN、root+旧 ed25519 仍可登（机器没下线）
Host wunoos-old
  HostName 119.8.167.61
  User root
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519_wunoos_119
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ServerAliveInterval 60
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

# 注意：climming-remote-tunnel 仍指向旧机 119.8.167.61（另一条业务，本次未动）

Host climming-remote-tunnel
  HostName 119.8.167.61
  User root
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/climming_remote_gpu
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ExitOnForwardFailure yes
  ServerAliveInterval 30
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

# 荣耀 MagicBook 改装的 Ubuntu 24.04 开发机（主机名 Xaiver-linux，MAC 18:93:41:13:e4:7b）。
# linux-dev-lan 只是引导用的局域网别名，DHCP 会漂；ZeroTier 地址到位后改用 linux-dev。
# 保活调短是故意的：这台机空闲挂起时 TCP 不会立刻报错，长保活会让命令干等。
Host linux-dev-lan
  HostName 192.168.1.8
  User xaiver
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ConnectTimeout 25
  ServerAliveInterval 20
  ServerAliveCountMax 6
  ProxyCommand none
  ServerAliveInterval 20
  ServerAliveCountMax 3

# Host linux-dev
#   HostName <join 166359304e354777 并在 Central 授权后回填 10.111.127.x>
#   User xaiver
#   Port 22
#   IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
#   IdentitiesOnly yes
#   ProxyCommand none
#   ServerAliveInterval 20
#   ServerAliveCountMax 3

# 2026-10-06：mDNS 通道，DHCP 换了地址也不受影响（实测 xaiver-linux.local -> 192.168.1.8 由本机解析）
Host linux-dev-mdns
  HostName xaiver-linux.local
  User xaiver
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ConnectTimeout 25
  ServerAliveInterval 20
  # 本机把 .local 解析成一组地址、里面有够不着的全局 IPv6；默认地址族会先试它们 ⇒ 每次连多 ~10 秒。
  # 现量：默认 10.66s/10.67s，强制 inet 0.32-0.81s（2026-10-06 22:2x）。回退：删掉本行。
  AddressFamily inet

# tfa: 那台荣耀 Linux 开发机的规范别名（唯一改动点）
# 现在走 mDNS（不含局域网 IP 的数字，地址漂了也认）；
# ZeroTier 授权之后，把下面这行 HostName 换成该网络的托管地址（10.111.x）即可，其余不用动。
Host linux-dev
  HostName 10.111.127.12
  User xaiver
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ConnectTimeout 25
  ServerAliveInterval 20
  ServerAliveCountMax 6
  # 同上：.local 在本机被解析进一组含全局 IPv6 的地址，默认地址族要先等它们超时（实测 10.6s → 0.3-0.8s）。
  # ZeroTier 授权后 HostName 换成 10.111.x 的 IPv4，这一行照旧成立。回退：删掉本行。
  AddressFamily inet

# tfa 三机互通：Windows 的局域网退路（2026-10-07 加；回退＝删掉本段到文件末，备份见 ~/.ssh/config.tfa-mesh-bak-20261007）
Host windows-pc-lan
  HostName 192.168.1.2
  User 41478
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ConnectTimeout 10
  ProxyCommand none

# ── Hong Kong 服务器（2026-10-28 新增）─────────────────────────────────────
# 境外 Ubuntu 24.04 云主机，root 登录，仅密钥认证（一次性密码已废弃，见文末说明）。
Host hongkong
  HostName 45.207.214.159
  User root
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/hongkong_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  StrictHostKeyChecking accept-new
  ServerAliveInterval 30
  ServerAliveCountMax 3
  # 境外机器：如需走本地代理才能稳定连通，可临时加 ProxyCommand nc -X 5 -x 127.0.0.1:7890 %h %p
  # 目前实测直连 22 端口可达，故不设代理。

server {
    listen 80;
    server_name xiangleideng.site www.xiangleideng.site;
    if ($host = xiangleideng.site) { return 301 https://$host$request_uri; }

proxy_cache_bypass $http_upgrade;
    }

    # Blog API
    location /api/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8085;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    # Blog Admin
    location = /admin { return 302 /admin/login; }
    location = /admin/ { return 302 /admin/login; }
    location /admin {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8085;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    }

    location /static/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8085;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
    }

    # Blog Frontend (Nuxt3)
    location = /blog { return 301 /blog/; }
    location /blog/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3004;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_cache_bypass $http_upgrade;
    }

    # Redirect old blog routes
    location = /log { return 301 /blog/; }
    location /log/ { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /about { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /link { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /message { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /reward { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /_nuxt { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }

    # Uploads
    location /uploads/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8085;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
    }

    # Homepage 静态文件
    location / {
        root /opt/home/apps/homepage/dist;
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
        location ~* \.(js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|ico|svg|woff|woff2|ttf|eot|webp|gz|br)$ {
            expires 1y;
            add_header Cache-Control "public, immutable";
        }
    }

    add_header X-Frame-Options "SAMEORIGIN" always;
    add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
    add_header X-XSS-Protection "1; mode=block" always;
    add_header Referrer-Policy "strict-origin-when-cross-origin" always;

    access_log /var/log/nginx/xiangleideng.site.access.log;
    error_log  /var/log/nginx/xiangleideng.site.error.log;
}

===== scripts/deploy-local.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# 本地构建并上传部署。
# 使用前设置：DEPLOY_SSH_HOST、DEPLOY_SSH_USER、DEPLOY_SSH_PORT（可选）、
# DEPLOY_SSH_KEY（可选）。个人站部署目录固定为 /opt/home，不允许覆盖。

ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
DEPLOY_SITE_ID="personal-home"
EXPECTED_REMOTE_DIR="/opt/home"
REMOTE_DIR="${DEPLOY_REMOTE_DIR:-$EXPECTED_REMOTE_DIR}"
SSH_PORT="${DEPLOY_SSH_PORT:-22}"
SSH_ARGS=(
  -p "$SSH_PORT"
  -o ConnectTimeout=15
  -o ServerAliveInterval=15
  -o ServerAliveCountMax=10
)

if [[ -z "${DEPLOY_SSH_HOST:-}" || -z "${DEPLOY_SSH_USER:-}" ]]; then
  echo "缺少 DEPLOY_SSH_HOST 或 DEPLOY_SSH_USER。" >&2
  exit 1
fi

if [[ "$REMOTE_DIR" != "$EXPECTED_REMOTE_DIR" ]]; then
  echo "拒绝部署：个人站只能部署到 ${EXPECTED_REMOTE_DIR}，当前目标为 ${REMOTE_DIR}。" >&2
  exit 1
fi

if [[ -n "${DEPLOY_SSH_KEY:-}" ]]; then
  SSH_ARGS+=(-i "$DEPLOY_SSH_KEY")
fi

verify_remote_target() {
  ssh "${SSH_ARGS[@]}" "${DEPLOY_SSH_USER}@${DEPLOY_SSH_HOST}" \
    bash -s -- "$REMOTE_DIR" "$DEPLOY_SITE_ID" <<'REMOTE_CHECK'
set -euo pipefail

remote_dir="$1"
site_id="$2"

if [[ ! -d "${remote_dir}" ]]; then
  echo "拒绝部署：目标目录不存在：${remote_dir}" >&2
  exit 1
fi

resolved_dir="$(readlink -f "${remote_dir}")"
if [[ "$resolved_dir" != "/opt/home" ]]; then
  echo "拒绝部署：个人站目标解析为 ${resolved_dir}，而不是 /opt/home。" >&2
  exit 1
fi

marker="$remote_dir/.deploy-site-id"
if [[ -f "$marker" ]] && [[ "$(cat "$marker")" != "$site_id" ]]; then
  echo "拒绝部署：目标目录属于另一个站点（$(cat "$marker")）。" >&2
  exit 1
fi

printf '%s\n' "$site_id" > "$marker"
REMOTE_CHECK
}

cd "$ROOT_DIR"

echo "==> 校验个人站部署目标"
verify_remote_target

echo "==> 构建 homepage"
pnpm --filter homepage build
echo "==> 构建 blog-admin"
pnpm --filter blog-admin build:pro
echo "==> 构建 blog-frontend"
pnpm --filter blog-frontend build:pro
echo "==> 检查后端测试"
pnpm --filter blog-api test

echo "==> 单连接上传源码、构建产物并重启服务"
COPYFILE_DISABLE=1 tar --no-xattrs -czf - \
  --exclude='./.git' \
  --exclude='./.env' \
  --exclude='./.env.*' \
  --exclude='*/.env' \
  --exclude='*/.env.*' \
  --exclude='._*' \
  --exclude='*/._*' \
  --exclude='./node_modules' \
  --exclude='*/node_modules' \
  --exclude='*/.nuxt' \
  --exclude='*/logs' \
  --exclude='database*.db' \
  --exclude='*/database*.db' \
  -C "$ROOT_DIR" . \
  | ssh "${SSH_ARGS[@]}" "${DEPLOY_SSH_USER}@${DEPLOY_SSH_HOST}" \
    "mkdir -p '$REMOTE_DIR' && tar --no-overwrite-dir -xzf - -C '$REMOTE_DIR' && cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && bash deploy.sh --skip-build"

echo "本地上传部署完成。"

const nodemailer = require('nodemailer');
const config = require("config");
const mailConfig = config.get('mail')

let transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
    host: mailConfig.smtp_host, // 腾讯云SMTP服务器
	port: mailConfig.smtp_port,
	secure: true, // 使用SSL
	auth: {
		user: mailConfig.smtp_user, //SMTP用户名
        pass: mailConfig.smtp_password, // SMTP密码
	}
})

module.exports = transporter;

# 中央服务凭据（本目录不入 git）

这是中央身份服务发给 **xiangleideng.site** 的接入凭据。它由中央侧生成并登记，
本目录已加入 `.gitignore`，**不要提交、不要贴进聊天记录、不要发到前端**。

## 先读这一份

中央接入总文档（唯一入口）：

    https://gitee.com/dengxiadeng/x-creative-team/blob/dev/docs/integration/central-services.md

仓库内副本（如果这台机器上有 x-c 仓）：

    ../x-c/docs/integration/central-services.md

## 你能用的两样东西

| 能力 | 入口 | 凭据变量 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 滑块验证码 | `POST https://x-creative.team/api/captcha/v1/generate`、`POST https://x-creative.team/api/captcha/v1/match` | `CENTRAL_CAPTCHA_KEY` |
| 中央认证（OIDC） | `https://waytofuture.cn/api/oidc` | 见下方 OIDC 段（若为空说明还没注册） |

## 自检（先跑这个，再写业务代码）

    CENTRAL_CAPTCHA_KEY=$(grep '^CENTRAL_CAPTCHA_KEY=' .central/credentials.env | cut -d= -f2-) \

期望输出：`ok  401×2 / generate 200 (...) / match 200 success=false`

## 边界（很重要）

- 中央**不做**登录页，也**不接管**你的会话。你的登录界面和会话仍然是你自己的。
- 验证码必须从**你的服务端**调用，key 不能下发到浏览器（没有 CORS，浏览器直连会被拦）。
- 挑战一次性：答错也作废，必须重新取一张。

  "description": "Replace domain across repo with Python"
}

Because it ends with 'assets/assets'. My check was wrong.

#### Thinking

Now the Nginx step (step 5) — on the new server there's no nginx, Caddy is the reverse proxy. Options:
(a) Make step 5 handle both: if nginx present → reload; elif caddy present → `systemctl reload caddy` (not needed since we don't change Caddyfile per deploy, but harmless).
Actually per-deploy we don't touch Caddyfile at all. The homepage static files are served directly by Caddy's file_server from the dist dir, so no reload is needed. Keeping the nginx branch as-is yields a WARN each deploy — noisy but harmless. Better: replace Step 5 with a proxy-aware reload that only acts when the relevant binary exists.

I'll change Step 5 to: if nginx exists → test+reload; elif caddy exists → validate config (`caddy validate`) — no reload needed for static file changes; else warn "未找到 nginx/caddy，跳过静态层重载".

# 同一份 dist 要同时铺到 /admin 与 /mgmt：产物里的资源路径是绝对 /admin/assets，故两处共用一份。
sync_admin_dist() {
  if [ ! -d "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist" ]; then
    warn "未找到 blog-admin 构建产物，跳过后台静态文件同步"
    return
  fi
  for target in admin mgmt; do
    mkdir -p "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target"
    rm -rf "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target/"*
    cp -r "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist/"* "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target/"
  done
  log "已同步 blog-admin 构建产物到 public/admin 与 public/mgmt"
}

const redisService = require("../services/redisService");

const config = require("config");

const getMailSender = () => {
  const mailConfig = config.get("mail");
  const email = mailConfig.from_email || mailConfig.smtp_user;
  const name = mailConfig.from_name || config.get("author.name");
  return `${name}<${email}>`;
};

    const email = String(question?.contact || "").trim();

# 荣耀 MagicBook 改装的 Ubuntu 24.04 开发机（主机名 Xaiver-linux，MAC 18:93:41:13:e4:7b）。

  ServerAliveInterval 20
  # 本机把 .local 解析成一组地址、里面有够不着的全局 IPv6；默认地址族会先试它们 ⇒ 每次连多 ~10 秒。
  # 现量：默认 10.66s/10.67s，强制 inet 0.32-0.81s（2026-10-06 22:2x）。回退：删掉本行。
  AddressFamily inet

# tfa: 那台荣耀 Linux 开发机的规范别名（唯一改动点）
# 现在走 mDNS（不含局域网 IP 的数字，地址漂了也认）；
# ZeroTier 授权之后，把下面这行 HostName 换成该网络的托管地址（10.111.x）即可，其余不用动。
Host linux-dev
  HostName 10.111.127.12
  User xaiver
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ConnectTimeout 25
  ServerAliveInterval 20
  ServerAliveCountMax 6
  # 同上：.local 在本机被解析进一组含全局 IPv6 的地址，默认地址族要先等它们超时（实测 10.6s → 0.3-0.8s）。
  # ZeroTier 授权后 HostName 换成 10.111.x 的 IPv4，这一行照旧成立。回退：删掉本行。
  AddressFamily inet

# tfa 三机互通：Windows 的局域网退路（2026-10-07 加；回退＝删掉本段到文件末，备份见 ~/.ssh/config.tfa-mesh-bak-20261007）
Host windows-pc-lan
  HostName 192.168.1.2
  User 41478
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/id_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  ConnectTimeout 10
  ProxyCommand none

# ── Hong Kong 服务器（2026-10-28 新增）─────────────────────────────────────
# 境外 Ubuntu 24.04 云主机，root 登录，仅密钥认证（一次性密码已废弃，见文末说明）。
Host hongkong
  HostName 45.207.214.159
  User root
  Port 22
  IdentityFile ~/.ssh/hongkong_ed25519
  IdentitiesOnly yes
  StrictHostKeyChecking accept-new
  ServerAliveInterval 30
  ServerAliveCountMax 3
  # 境外机器：如需走本地代理才能稳定连通，可临时加 ProxyCommand nc -X 5 -x 127.0.0.1:7890 %h %p
  # 目前实测直连 22 端口可达，故不设代理。

server {
    listen 80;
    server_name xiangleideng.site www.xiangleideng.site;
    if ($host = xiangleideng.site) { return 301 https://$host$request_uri; }
    if ($host = www.xiangleideng.site) { return 301 https://xiangleideng.site$request_uri; }
    return 404;
}
server {
    listen 443 ssl;
    server_name www.xiangleideng.site;
    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/xiangleideng.site/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/xiangleideng.site/privkey.pem;
    include /etc/letsencrypt/options-ssl-nginx.conf;
    ssl_dhparam /etc/letsencrypt/ssl-dhparams.pem;
    return 301 https://xiangleideng.site$request_uri;
}

    location = /admin/ { return 302 /admin/login; }
    location /admin {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8085;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    }

    location /static/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8085;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
    }

    # Blog Frontend (Nuxt3)
    location = /blog { return 301 /blog/; }
    location /blog/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3004;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_cache_bypass $http_upgrade;
    }

    # Redirect old blog routes
    location = /log { return 301 /blog/; }
    location /log/ { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /about { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /link { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /message { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /reward { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }
    location /_nuxt { return 301 /blog$request_uri; }

    # Uploads
    location /uploads/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8085;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
    }

    # Homepage 静态文件
    location / {
        root /opt/home/apps/homepage/dist;
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
        location ~* \.(js|css|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|ico|svg|woff|woff2|ttf|eot|webp|gz|br)$ {
            expires 1y;
            add_header Cache-Control "public, immutable";
        }
    }

    add_header X-Frame-Options "SAMEORIGIN" always;
    add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
    add_header X-XSS-Protection "1; mode=block" always;
    add_header Referrer-Policy "strict-origin-when-cross-origin" always;

    access_log /var/log/nginx/xiangleideng.site.access.log;
    error_log  /var/log/nginx/xiangleideng.site.error.log;
}

===== scripts/deploy-local.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# 本地构建并上传部署。
# 使用前设置：DEPLOY_SSH_HOST、DEPLOY_SSH_USER、DEPLOY_SSH_PORT（可选）、
# DEPLOY_SSH_KEY（可选）。个人站部署目录固定为 /opt/home，不允许覆盖。

ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
DEPLOY_SITE_ID="personal-home"
EXPECTED_REMOTE_DIR="/opt/home"
REMOTE_DIR="${DEPLOY_REMOTE_DIR:-$EXPECTED_REMOTE_DIR}"
SSH_PORT="${DEPLOY_SSH_PORT:-22}"
SSH_ARGS=(
  -p "$SSH_PORT"
  -o ConnectTimeout=15
  -o ServerAliveInterval=15
  -o ServerAliveCountMax=10
)

if [[ -z "${DEPLOY_SSH_HOST:-}" || -z "${DEPLOY_SSH_USER:-}" ]]; then
  echo "缺少 DEPLOY_SSH_HOST 或 DEPLOY_SSH_USER。" >&2
  exit 1
fi

if [[ "$REMOTE_DIR" != "$EXPECTED_REMOTE_DIR" ]]; then
  echo "拒绝部署：个人站只能部署到 ${EXPECTED_REMOTE_DIR}，当前目标为 ${REMOTE_DIR}。" >&2
  exit 1
fi

if [[ -n "${DEPLOY_SSH_KEY:-}" ]]; then
  SSH_ARGS+=(-i "$DEPLOY_SSH_KEY")
fi

verify_remote_target() {
  ssh "${SSH_ARGS[@]}" "${DEPLOY_SSH_USER}@${DEPLOY_SSH_HOST}" \
    bash -s -- "$REMOTE_DIR" "$DEPLOY_SITE_ID" <<'REMOTE_CHECK'
set -euo pipefail

remote_dir="$1"
site_id="$2"

if [[ ! -d "${remote_dir}" ]]; then
  echo "拒绝部署：目标目录不存在：${remote_dir}" >&2
  exit 1
fi

resolved_dir="$(readlink -f "${remote_dir}")"
if [[ "$resolved_dir" != "/opt/home" ]]; then
  echo "拒绝部署：个人站目标解析为 ${resolved_dir}，而不是 /opt/home。" >&2
  exit 1
fi

marker="$remote_dir/.deploy-site-id"
if [[ -f "$marker" ]] && [[ "$(cat "$marker")" != "$site_id" ]]; then
  echo "拒绝部署：目标目录属于另一个站点（$(cat "$marker")）。" >&2
  exit 1
fi

printf '%s\n' "$site_id" > "$marker"
REMOTE_CHECK
}

cd "$ROOT_DIR"

echo "==> 校验个人站部署目标"
verify_remote_target

echo "==> 构建 homepage"
pnpm --filter homepage build
echo "==> 构建 blog-admin"
pnpm --filter blog-admin build:pro
echo "==> 构建 blog-frontend"
pnpm --filter blog-frontend build:pro
echo "==> 检查后端测试"
pnpm --filter blog-api test

echo "==> 单连接上传源码、构建产物并重启服务"
COPYFILE_DISABLE=1 tar --no-xattrs -czf - \
  --exclude='./.git' \
  --exclude='./.env' \
  --exclude='./.env.*' \
  --exclude='*/.env' \
  --exclude='*/.env.*' \
  --exclude='._*' \
  --exclude='*/._*' \
  --exclude='./node_modules' \
  --exclude='*/node_modules' \
  --exclude='*/.nuxt' \
  --exclude='*/logs' \
  --exclude='database*.db' \
  --exclude='*/database*.db' \
  -C "$ROOT_DIR" . \
  | ssh "${SSH_ARGS[@]}" "${DEPLOY_SSH_USER}@${DEPLOY_SSH_HOST}" \
    "mkdir -p '$REMOTE_DIR' && tar --no-overwrite-dir -xzf - -C '$REMOTE_DIR' && cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && bash deploy.sh --skip-build"

echo "本地上传部署完成。"

const nodemailer = require('nodemailer');
const config = require("config");
const mailConfig = config.get('mail')

let transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
    host: mailConfig.smtp_host, // 腾讯云SMTP服务器
	port: mailConfig.smtp_port,
	secure: true, // 使用SSL
	auth: {
		user: mailConfig.smtp_user, //SMTP用户名
        pass: mailConfig.smtp_password, // SMTP密码
	}
})

module.exports = transporter;

app.use("/api/article", articleRouter);
app.use("/api/user", userRouter);
app.use("/api/storage", storageRouter);
app.use("/api/admin", adminRouter);
app.use("/api/comment", commentRouter);
app.use("/api/friendLink",friendLinkRouter)
app.use("/api/question",questionRouter)
app.use('/api/configuration',configurationRouter)
app.use('/api/weather', weatherRouter); // 天气API路由

// --错误处理--

// JWT 验证错误处理器

// 404 处理中间件

  next(createError(404));
});

app.use(function (err, req, res, next) {
  const utils = require("./utils/index");
  // set locals, only providing error in development
  res.locals.message = err.message;
  res.locals.error = req.app.get("env") === "development" ? err : {};

  console.log('全局错误处理器捕捉到错误：',err);

**Response:**

# 中央服务凭据（本目录不入 git）

这是中央身份服务发给 **xiangleideng.site** 的接入凭据。它由中央侧生成并登记，
本目录已加入 `.gitignore`，**不要提交、不要贴进聊天记录、不要发到前端**。

## 先读这一份

中央接入总文档（唯一入口）：

    https://gitee.com/dengxiadeng/x-creative-team/blob/dev/docs/integration/central-services.md

仓库内副本（如果这台机器上有 x-c 仓）：

    ../x-c/docs/integration/central-services.md

## 你能用的两样东西

| 能力 | 入口 | 凭据变量 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 滑块验证码 | `POST https://x-creative.team/api/captcha/v1/generate`、`POST https://x-creative.team/api/captcha/v1/match` | `CENTRAL_CAPTCHA_KEY` |
| 中央认证（OIDC） | `https://waytofuture.cn/api/oidc` | 见下方 OIDC 段（若为空说明还没注册） |

## 自检（先跑这个，再写业务代码）

    CENTRAL_CAPTCHA_KEY=$(grep '^CENTRAL_CAPTCHA_KEY=' .central/credentials.env | cut -d= -f2-) \
    CAPTCHA_KEY="$CENTRAL_CAPTCHA_KEY" node ../x-c/services/captcha/scripts/smoke.mjs

期望输出：`ok  401×2 / generate 200 (...) / match 200 success=false`

## 边界（很重要）

- 中央**不做**登录页，也**不接管**你的会话。你的登录界面和会话仍然是你自己的。
- 验证码必须从**你的服务端**调用，key 不能下发到浏览器（没有 CORS，浏览器直连会被拦）。
- 挑战一次性：答错也作废，必须重新取一张。

## 已登记的 OIDC 客户端

- `home` → `https://xiangleideng.site/api/auth/oidc/callback`（中央侧 active=false，且域名已过期）

## 中央 AI

该站不是中央 AI 的登记产品。
文档：`docs/integration/central-ai.md`（x-c 仓）。

{
  "command": "cd \"/Users/rocalight/Desktop/All in one Data/01_PROJECTS/home\" && python3 - <<'PY'\nimport os, sys\n\nSKIP_DIRS = {'.git', 'node_modules', '.claude', 'graphify-out', '.output', 'dist', '.nuxt', '.next'}\n# stale committed admin build: replaced by a fresh build at deploy time\nSKIP_FILES = set()\n\npairs = [\n    (r'xiangleideng\\.site', r'xaiverdeng\\.site'),   # regex-escaped form inside test literals\n    ('xiangleideng.site', 'xaiverdeng.site'),\n]\n\nchanged, skipped_binary, leftovers = [], [], []\nfor root, dirs, files in os.walk('.'):\n    dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in SKIP_DIRS]\n    if os.path.join(root, 'assets') .endswith('public/mgmt/assets'):\n        continue\n    for name in files:\n        p = os.path.join(root, name)\n        try:\n            if os.path.getsize(p) > 3_000_000:\n                continue\n            with open(p, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                text = f.read()\n        except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):\n            skipped_binary.append(p)\n            continue\n        if 'xiangleideng' not in text:\n            continue\n        new = text\n        for a, b in pairs:\n            new = new.replace(a, b)\n        if new != text:\n            with open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                f.write(new)\n            changed.append(p)\n        if 'xiangleideng' in new:\n            leftovers.append(p)\n\nprint(\"changed files:\", len(changed))\nprint(\"unparseable/binary:\", len(skipped_binary))\nprint(\"still containing bare 'xiangleideng' after replace:\", len(leftovers))\nfor p in leftovers:\n    print(\"   \", p)\nPY",
  "description": "Replace domain across repo with Python"
}

56	echo "" | tee -a "$LOG_FILE"
57	log "========== CI/CD 自动部署开始 =========="

59	if [ "$SKIP_BUILD" = true ]; then
60	  log "跳过 Git 同步和构建步骤 (--skip-build，使用本地上传的文件)"
61	  if [ -d "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist" ]; then

  "new_string": "verify_deploy_target\n\n# blog-admin 产物同时挂 /admin 与 /mgmt：blog-api 的 express.static 直接服务这两个目录，\n# 构建产物里 index.html 引用的绝对路径是 /admin/assets，所以同一份 dist 复制到两处都能工作。\nsync_admin_dist() {\n  if [ ! -d \"$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist\" ]; then\n    warn \"未找到 blog-admin 构建产物，跳过后台静态文件同步\"\n    return\n  fi\n  for target in admin mgmt; do\n    mkdir -p \"$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target\"\n    rm -rf \"$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target/\"*\n    cp -r \"$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist/\"* \"$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target/\"\n  done\n  log \"已同步 blog-admin 构建产物到 public/admin 与 public/mgmt\"\n}\n\n# 解析参数",
  "old_string": "verify_deploy_target\n\n# 解析参数"
}

# 同一份 dist 要同时铺到 /admin 与 /mgmt：产物里的资源路径是绝对 /admin/assets，故两处共用一份。
sync_admin_dist() {
  if [ ! -d "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist" ]; then
    warn "未找到 blog-admin 构建产物，跳过后台静态文件同步"
    return
  fi
  for target in admin mgmt; do
    mkdir -p "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target"
    rm -rf "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target/"*
    cp -r "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-admin/dist/"* "$REPO_DIR/apps/blog-api/public/$target/"
  done
  log "已同步 blog-admin 构建产物到 public/admin 与 public/mgmt"
}

const transporter = require("../db/mail");
const redisService = require("../services/redisService");
const utils = require("../utils/index");
const config = require("config");

const getMailSender = () => {
  const mailConfig = config.get("mail");
  const email = mailConfig.from_email || mailConfig.smtp_user;
  const name = mailConfig.from_name || config.get("author.name");
  return `${name}<${email}>`;
};

module.exports = {
  // 匿名树洞回复通知。只有联系方式是邮箱时才发送，微信等其他联系方式不触发邮件。
  sendQuestionAnswerMail: async (question) => {
    const email = String(question?.contact || "").trim();
    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) return false;

    const trackingCode = encodeURIComponent(question.trackingCode);
    const answerUrl = `${config.get("author.website")}/blog/message?tab=ask&trackingCode=${trackingCode}`;

      from: getMailSender(),
      to: email,
      subject: `你的匿名树洞有新回复（${question.trackingCode}）`,

        <div style="font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'PingFang SC', sans-serif; line-height: 1.7; color: #17201d;">

        </div>
      `,
    };

  // --获取--

  getAllUser: async (req, res) => {
    res.json(
      await userService.getAllUser(req.params.currentPage, req.params.pageSize)
    );
  },
  // 分页 筛选搜索用户

        req.body.pageSize

    );
